Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The tissue variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 374 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 874 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to tissue structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 374/874 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in tissue theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 665 and the invariant I(A,B) = 146 mod 3.

Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
